# Codex conversation 01a0a5e8-ff60-7b41-88e0-0879738408bf

## assistant

<proposed_plan>
# LSAT U booking confirmation upgrade

## Summary

Show confirmation immediately after payment, then update it automatically when Google finishes creating and sending the invitation. Replace the sparse page with a Calendly-inspired LSAT U confirmation card, including the requested corner ribbon and a calendar-only action.

## Implementation changes

- Start from the latest remote `main`, preserving the existing dirty local branch and Lovable’s recent changes.
- Expand the protected booking-status response with:
  - Attendee name
  - Session title
  - Start and end time
  - Duration
  - Booking timezone
  - Calendar readiness
  - Google Calendar event URL
- Persist the timezone selected during booking. Existing bookings fall back to the browser timezone, then `America/Toronto`.
- Show the final confirmation page immediately:
  - Initially: “You are scheduled!” and “We’re preparing your calendar invitation.”
  - Continue polling while Google Calendar finishes.
  - Once ready: “A calendar invitation has been sent to your email address.”
  - Enable one primary “Open Invitation” button linked to the verified Google Calendar event.
  - If preparation is delayed, keep the booking confirmed and show a truthful recovery message instead of spinning indefinitely.
- Build the meeting-details card with the session title, attendee name, localized date and time, duration, timezone, and a note that Google Meet details are included in the calendar invitation.
- Add a diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” corner ribbon. Do not reproduce Calendly trademarks, assets, menu, or copy-link controls.
- Keep the confirmation page calendar-only, with no separate Meet button.
- Correct the email wording to past tense: the invitation “has been sent,” not “is on its way.” Retain the useful Meet action in the email.
- Replace the hardcoded 70-minute email duration and Toronto timezone with values from the booking.
- Update the outbox worker to perform a bounded second claim after calendar creation, allowing newly unblocked confirmation emails to send during the same webhook-triggered run instead of waiting up to one minute.
- Preserve the required ordering: payment finalized, calendar event and Meet created, Google invitation sent, confirmation email sent. Do not claim that Google has placed the event directly onto every recipient’s calendar because recipient settings can still require acceptance.

## Public interface changes

- Extend the capability-protected status payload with `attendee_name`, `event_label`, `duration_minutes`, `timezone`, `calendar_ready`, and `calendar_url`.
- Add a validated IANA timezone to the booking hold/finalization data.
- Resolve `calendar_url` from the mirrored Google event only after validating the booking capability token.

## Test and acceptance plan

- Test immediate booked rendering followed by automatic calendar-ready updating.
- Test calendar button enablement, delayed-calendar recovery, expired tokens, and unauthorized status requests.
- Test dynamic session titles, durations, timezones, and daylight-saving formatting.
- Test that Google promotion completes before email eligibility and that the same worker invocation drains newly unblocked email jobs without duplicates.
- Render-test the email to confirm the corrected wording and accurate booking details.
- Browser-test narrow, medium, wide, and side-panel layouts, including ribbon placement, keyboard access, focus states, and screen-reader labels.
- After deployment, run one separately authorized $1 live booking and verify the Stripe payment, confirmation page, Google invitation, Meet details, and confirmation email end to end.

## Assumptions

- The requested “automatic” behavior means LSAT U sends the invitation immediately. Google still controls whether an external invitation appears automatically or awaits acceptance.
- Deploying the Edge Function through Lovable requires Joshua’s explicit confirmation immediately before any action that may spend Lovable credits.
- No real payment or Lovable credit will be used during implementation without that fresh confirmation.
</proposed_plan>

<proposed_plan>
# Match the Calendly confirmation experience with LSAT U branding

## Summary

Use the supplied screenshot as the visual source of truth for both the confirmation email and the booking-success page. Preserve its restrained layout and information hierarchy, replacing every Calendly element with LSAT U branding and real booking data.

## Confirmation email

- Send from `LSAT U <noreply@lsatprep.study>`.
- Replace the current decorative gold confirmation card with the screenshot’s clean, white, centered email layout.
- Reproduce the visible structure:
  - Centered LSAT U logo
  - Light dotted divider
  - Left-aligned content column
  - `Hi [first name],`
  - `Your LSAT U session has been scheduled.`
  - Bold field labels with values beneath them
  - Event type
  - Invitee name
  - Invitee email
  - Date and time
  - Duration
  - Timezone
  - Location: Google Meet
- Use LSAT U’s existing typography and colors while matching the screenshot’s generous whitespace, narrow content width, light dividers, and minimal styling.
- Keep a Google Meet button lower in the email, followed by plain fallback links for clients that block styled buttons.
- Change the inaccurate sentence to: “Your Google Calendar invitation has been sent.”
- Remove “is on its way” and do not imply that the LSAT U confirmation email is waiting for calendar creation.
- Populate duration and timezone from the booking instead of hardcoding 70 minutes and Toronto.
- Build with email-safe tables and inline styles so Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, and mobile clients remain visually consistent.

## Gmail event panel

- Continue creating the real Google Calendar event with the invitee as an attendee, Google Meet attached, and `sendUpdates: "all"`.
- Add validated `EventReservation` structured metadata to the LSAT U confirmation email with the event title, attendee, start time, location, reservation number, and secure reservation URL.
- Add a Gmail `ViewAction` targeting the booking’s protected reservation page.
- Validate the markup with Google’s Email Markup Tester.
- Prepare the required Google sender-registration submission using the production sender, matching SPF or DKIM, `From`, and return-path domains. Google requires registration before marked-up production emails reliably receive enhanced Gmail treatment. [Google registration requirements](https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/registering-with-google)
- Treat Gmail’s blue event panel as an enhancement, not part of the HTML email. Gmail controls its exact appearance, labels, conflict warnings, and available buttons. LSAT U can provide the correct event metadata but cannot force Gmail to render an identical panel. [EventReservation specification](https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/types/EventReservation)

## Confirmation page

- Show “You are scheduled!” immediately after payment confirmation.
- Mirror the earlier Calendly confirmation-page structure:
  - Sent-calendar-invitation message
  - Calendar-only primary action
  - Session-details panel
  - Attendee name
  - Localized date and time
  - Duration
  - Timezone
  - Google Meet availability note
  - Diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” corner ribbon
- While Google is still creating the event, show “Preparing your calendar invitation” and keep the calendar action disabled.
- Continue polling after the booking becomes `booked`.
- Replace the preparing state automatically with “A calendar invitation has been sent to your email address” and enable “Open Invitation” when the Google event URL becomes available.
- If Google is delayed, keep the booking confirmed and display a recovery message instead of spinning indefinitely.

## Backend and interface changes

- Persist the timezone selected during booking.
- Extend the capability-protected status response with `attendee_name`, `event_label`, `duration_minutes`, `timezone`, `calendar_ready`, and `calendar_url`.
- Resolve `calendar_url` from the mirrored Google event only after validating the booking token.
- Make the outbox worker claim newly unblocked confirmation-email jobs again after calendar promotion, so the email can send in the same webhook-triggered run.
- Preserve idempotency and the required ordering: payment finalized, calendar and Meet created, Google invitation sent, confirmation email sent.

## Verification

- Snapshot-test the email at desktop and mobile widths against the supplied reference.
- Render-test Gmail, Outlook, and Apple Mail output.
- Test real booking values, long names, different durations, multiple timezones, daylight-saving transitions, and missing optional notes.
- Test the immediate confirmation page, calendar-preparing transition, successful calendar link, delayed-calendar recovery, and invalid booking tokens.
- Verify the structured metadata with Google’s official validator.
- After deployment, perform one separately authorized $1 production booking and verify Stripe payment, calendar invitation, Meet link, Gmail presentation, confirmation page, and LSAT U email.
- Lovable deployment or any action that may consume Lovable credits requires Joshua’s fresh confirmation immediately before execution.

## Assumptions

- “Exactly” means faithfully reproducing the reference email’s layout and hierarchy with LSAT U branding and booking-specific content.
- Gmail’s surrounding interface and generated blue event panel cannot be pixel-controlled by the email template.
- Google may still require the recipient to accept an external invitation depending on that recipient’s Calendar settings.
</proposed_plan>

# LSAT U burgundy booking confirmation

## Summary

Match the supplied Calendly reference using LSAT U branding and its existing burgundy palette. The email body and confirmation page will use burgundy instead of blue. Gmail’s generated event panel may remain blue because Google controls that interface.

## Visual specification

- Use LSAT U’s production booking color:
  - Primary: `hsl(347 70% 27%)`, approximately `#75152A`
  - Dark gradient: `#4F0D1C → #6E1423 → #8E1D3A`
  - White button and ribbon text
- Use exact inline hex colors in email HTML because email clients cannot access the website’s CSS variables.
- Preserve the reference’s white background, generous spacing, narrow content column, understated dividers, and simple typography.
- Replace the blue Calendly logo with the LSAT U mark in burgundy.
- Use burgundy for buttons, links, important icons, and the “Powered by LSAT U” ribbon.
- Do not make the entire email burgundy or reproduce any Calendly branding.

## Confirmation email

- Recreate the reference structure:
  - Centered burgundy LSAT U logo
  - Light divider
  - `Hi [first name],`
  - `Your LSAT U session has been scheduled.`
  - Event type
  - Invitee name and email
  - Date, time, duration, and timezone
  - Google Meet location
- Change the outdated message to: “Your Google Calendar invitation has been sent.”
- Keep a burgundy Google Meet button and plain fallback link.
- Use booking-derived duration and timezone instead of hardcoded values.
- Build with email-safe tables and inline styles for Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, and mobile clients.

## Gmail event panel

- Continue sending the actual Google Calendar invitation automatically with the attendee and Meet link.
- Add validated `EventReservation` metadata and a secure reservation action.
- Register the production sender with Google if LSAT U qualifies for enhanced Gmail markup.
- Accept that Gmail controls the generated panel’s blue background, button labels, conflict warnings, and final appearance. LSAT U cannot recolor Gmail’s own interface.

## Confirmation page

- Show “You are scheduled!” immediately after payment.
- Use the same burgundy palette for the success icon, calendar button, links, borders, and diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” ribbon.
- Display attendee, session type, date, time, duration, timezone, and conferencing information.
- Provide one calendar-only “Open Invitation” action.
- Continue polling while the calendar invitation is prepared, then enable the button automatically.
- Show a recoverable confirmed state if Google Calendar is delayed.

## Backend and verification

- Persist the selected timezone.
- Extend the protected booking-status response with the display details, calendar readiness, and calendar URL.
- Allow the outbox worker to send newly unblocked confirmation emails during the same run after calendar creation.
- Preserve payment, calendar, invitation, and email idempotency.
- Compare desktop and mobile email renders against the supplied screenshot.
- Test Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, different durations and timezones, delayed calendar creation, and invalid booking tokens.
- Perform one separately authorized $1 production booking after deployment.
- Obtain fresh confirmation before any Lovable action that could consume credits.

## Assumptions

- “Instead of blue” applies to all LSAT U-controlled email and confirmation-page elements.
- Gmail’s surrounding interface and generated event panel are excluded because their colors cannot be controlled by LSAT U.
</proposed_plan>

Use the absolute paths above.\n\n## Project Map\n\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\n\n## Stack And Commands\n\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\n- Build: `npm run build`\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\n- Test: `npm run test`\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\n\n## Working Rules\n\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\n\n## Product Constraints\n\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\n\n## Smart Drill Note\n\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\n\n## Verification (must not skip)\n\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\n\n## Workflow Expectations\n\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\n\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\n\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\n\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\n\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\n\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\n\n## Codex Adapter Layout\n\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\n\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\n\n## Known Gaps\n\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\n\n## Agent skills\n\n### Issue tracker\n\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n\n### Triage labels\n\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\n\n### Domain docs\n\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\n\r\n'}]

_did_ help Ann, because she went from a 140 to a 160, whereas Jerry just went from a 150 to 160.\\n\\n- **would have been even worse without**  \\n  the stimulus bill _did_ help, because the economy was in such a free-fall that if it hadn\'t been for the stimulus bill, the unemployment would have been at like 12%\\n\\n- **overlooked consideration**  \\n  even though it looks like the stimulus Only the caller\r\n * that minted this token may cancel with it, so a replay from a different\r\n * request gets nothing rather than someone else\'s token.\r\n */\r\nfunction responseBookingTokenForFree(\r\n  booking: { booking_token_hash?: string | null },\r\n  bookingTokenHash: string,\r\n  bookingToken: string,\r\n): string {\r\n  return booking.booking_token_hash === bookingTokenHash ? bookingToken : "";\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Is the host\'s calendar actually free for this booking\'s window, ignoring the\r\n * booking\'s own tentative event. Mirrors the check the Stripe webhook runs\r\n * before it promotes a paid hold.\r\n */\r\nasync function calendarClearFor(supabase: any, booking: any): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const { data: state, error } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state")\r\n    .select("calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  const ids = [...new Set([state?.calendar_id || "primary", ...(state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? [])])];\r\n  for (const calendar of ids) {\r\n    const page = await listEvents(calendar, {\r\n      timeMin: booking.start_at,\r\n      timeMax: booking.end_at,\r\n      singleEvents: "true",\r\n      showDeleted: "false",\r\n      maxResults: "50",\r\n    });\r\n    if (hasConflictingCalendarEvent(page?.items ?? [], booking.start_at, booking.end_at, booking.gcal_event_id)) {\r\n      return false;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  return true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\r\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\r\n    status,\r\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" },\r\n  });\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\r\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\r\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\r\n  return value;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function readBody(req: Request): Promise<any> {\r\n  const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\r\n  if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");\r\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await req.arrayBuffer());\r\n  if (bytes.byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error("payload_too_large");\r\n  return bytes.byteLength ? JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) : {};\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction clientIp(req: Request): string {\r\n  const platformIp = req.headers.get("cf-connecting-ip") || req.headers.get("fly-client-ip") || req.headers.get("x-real-ip");\r\n  if (platformIp?.trim()) return platformIp.trim();\r\n  const hops = (req.headers.get("x-forwarded-for") || "").split(",").map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);\r\n  return hops.at(-1) || "unknown";\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function verifyTurnstile(token: string, ip: string): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const secret = requiredEnv("TURNSTILE_SECRET");\r\n  if (!token) return false;\r\n  const response = await fetch("https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify", {\r\n    method: "POST",\r\n    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },\r\n    body: new URLSearchParams({ secret, response: token, remoteip: ip }),\r\n  });\r\n  if (!response.ok) return false;\r\n  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\r\n  return result?.success === true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function rateLimit(supabase: any, ipHash: string, action: string, limit: number): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("bump_booking_hit", {\r\n    _ip_hash: ipHash,\r\n    _slug: "single-session",\r\n    _action: action,\r\n  });\r\n  // Fail open. This is a throttle, not a gate: a counter that cannot write is\r\n  // a reason to log loudly, never a reason to stop every student from booking\r\n  // a session. Throwing here took the whole booking page down.\r\n  if (error) {\r\n    console.error("single-session rate limit unavailable, allowing", {\r\n      action,\r\n      code: error.code,\r\n      message: error.message,\r\n      details: error.details,\r\n    });\r\n    return true;\r\n  }\r\n  return Number(data ?? 0) <= limit;\r\n}\r\n\r\n/** True only for a caller holding a valid admin JWT. Used to decide whether an\r\n *  error response may carry its real cause. */\r\nasync function callerIsAdmin(req: Request): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  try {\r\n    const authorization = [REDACTED] || "";\r\n    if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) return false;\r\n    const admin = createClient(\r\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL") ?? "",\r\n      Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") ?? "",\r\n    );\r\n    const caller = await userFromRequest(req);\r\n    if (!caller) return false;\r\n    const { data: role } = await admin\r\n      .from("user_roles")\r\n      .select("role")\r\n      .eq("user_id", caller.id)\r\n      .eq("role", "admin")\r\n      .maybeSingle();\r\n    return !!role;\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return false;\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function optionalUserId(supabase: any, req: Request): Promise<string | null> {\r\n  const authorization = [REDACTED] || "";\r\n  if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) return null;\r\n  const caller = await userFromRequest(req);\r\n  return caller?.id ?? null;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction checkoutDescription(startAt: string, timezone: string): string {\r\n  let tz = timezone || SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone;\r\n  try { new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: tz }).format(); }\r\n  catch (_) { tz = SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone; }\r\n  const formatted = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\r\n    timeZone: tz,\r\n    weekday: "long",\r\n    month: "long",\r\n    day: "numeric",\r\n    hour: "numeric",\r\n    minute: "2-digit",\r\n  }).format(new Date(startAt));\r\n  return `LSAT Session with Joshua — ${formatted} (${tz})`;\r\n}\r\n\r\nfunction bookingTokenFromUrl(value: string): string | null {\r\n  try { return new URL(value).searchParams.get("booking_token"); }\r\n  catch (_) { return null; }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function calendarId(supabase: any): Promise<string> {\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function adoptTentativeEvent(calendar: string, bookingId: string, startAt: string, endAt: string): Promise<any> {\r\n  const eventId = deterministicSingleSessionEventId(bookingId);\r\n  let event: any = null;\r\n  try {\r\n    event = await insertEvent(calendar, {\r\n      id: eventId,\r\n      summary: "Reserved LSAT session",\r\n      description: "Temporary booking hold.",\r\n      start: { dateTime: startAt },\r\n      end: { dateTime: endAt },\r\n      transparency: "opaque",\r\n      visibility: "private",\r\n      extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_single_session_booking_id: bookingId, lsatu_hold: "true" } },\r\n    }, { sendUpdates: "none" });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    try { event = await getEvent(calendar, eventId); }\r\n    catch (_) {\r\n      const found = await listEvents(calendar, {\r\n        privateExtendedProperty: `lsatu_single_session_booking_id=${bookingId}`,\r\n        showDeleted: "false",\r\n        maxResults: "2",\r\n      });\r\n      event = found?.items?.find((item: any) => item.status !== "cancelled") ?? null;\r\n    }\r\n    if (!event) throw error;\r\n  }\r\n  return event;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function cleanupTentative(calendar: string, bookingId: string, knownEventId?: string | null) {\r\n  const eventId = knownEventId || deterministicSingleSessionEventId(bookingId);\r\n  try { await deleteEvent(calendar, eventId, { sendUpdates: "none" }); }\r\n  catch (error) { console.error("single-session calendar cleanup failed", { bookingId, eventId, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) }); }\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * The event this request is about. Defaults to the single session so an older\r\n * client that sends no slug keeps working exactly as it did.\r\n */\r\nasync function loadEvent(supabase: any, slug: unknown): Promise<BookingEventConfig> {\r\n  const wanted = String(slug || "single-session").trim().slice(0, 64) || "single-session";\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n    .from("booking_event_types")\r\n    .select("*")\r\n    .eq("slug", wanted)\r\n    .eq("active", true)\r\n    .maybeSingle();\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  if (!data) {\r\n    // An unknown slug must not silently fall back to the paid session.\r\n    if (wanted !== "single-session") throw new Error("unknown_event");\r\n    return SINGLE_SESSION_EVENT;\r\n  }\r\n  return toEventConfig(data);\r\n}\r\n\r\n/**\r\n * Has this one-off link already been used. Ordinary events are never spent, so\r\n * this is a single indexed lookup that answers false for every existing caller.\r\n */\r\nasync function linkSpent(supabase: any, event: BookingEventConfig): Promise<boolean> {\r\n  if (!event.singleUse) return false;\r\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("booking_event_is_spent", { _event_slug: event.slug });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data === true;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response("ok", { headers: corsHeaders });\r\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    const body = await readBody(req);\r\n    const action = String(body.action || "");\r\n    const supabaseUrl = requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL");\r\n    const serviceKey = requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");\r\n    const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey);\r\n    const ip = clientIp(req);\r\n    // Keyed when the secret is there, plain digest when it is not. Either way\r\n    // it is only a bucket key for counting hits, and a missing secret must not\r\n    // cost every student the ability to book.\r\n    const hmacKey = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_IP_HMAC_KEY");\r\n    if (!hmacKey) console.error("BOOKING_IP_HMAC_KEY is not set; throttle keys are unkeyed digests");\r\n    const ipHash = hmacKey ? await hmacHex(hmacKey, ip) : await sha256Hex(ip);\r\n    const isWrite = ["hold", "abandon", "cancel"].includes(action);\r\n    if (!await rateLimit(supabase, ipHash, isWrite ? action : "read", isWrite ? WRITE_LIMIT : READ_LIMIT)) {\r\n      return json({ error: "rate_limited" }, 429);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "info") {\r\n      const event = await loadEvent(supabase, body.event);\r\n      return json({\r\n        host: { name: "Joshua", title: event.label },\r\n        event: event.slug,\r\n        duration_minutes: event.lengthMinutes,\r\n        amount_cents: event.priceCents,\r\n        currency: event.currency,\r\n        requires_payment: event.requiresPayment,\r\n        timezone: SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone,\r\n        single_use: event.singleUse,\r\n        // A one-off link that has been used still resolves -- the page needs to\r\n        // say so rather than show an empty calendar. `note` is never included:\r\n        // it is Joshua\'s reminder of who the link was for.\r\n        available: !(await linkSpent(supabase, event)),\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "slots") {\r\n      const event = await loadEvent(supabase, body.event);\r\n      // A spent one-off link sells nothing. Checked before the cache, because\r\n      // whether a link is used changes on a booking, not on a 15-second timer.\r\n      if (await linkSpent(supabase, event)) return json({ slots: [], timezone: SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone });\r\n      // Keyed by event: a consultation and a session have different lengths,\r\n      // buffers and windows, so one cached answer cannot serve both.\r\n      const cached = slotsCache.get(event.slug);\r\n      if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) return json(cached.payload);\r\n      const nowMs = Date.now();\r\n      const { data: availability, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_booking_availability");\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      const { hostTz, rangesByDow, overridesByDate } = parseAvailability(availability);\r\n      // Load busy blocks for the whole window plus the buffers on both ends, so\r\n      // an event just outside the window still blocks the slot next to it.\r\n      const bufferMs = Math.max(event.bufferBeforeMinutes, event.bufferAfterMinutes) * 60_000;\r\n      const last = singleSessionWindowEndDate(nowMs, hostTz, event);\r\n      const rangeStart = new Date(nowMs + event.minimumNoticeMinutes * 60_000 - bufferMs).toISOString();\r\n      const rangeEnd = new Date(hostLocalToUtc(hostTz, last.year, last.month, last.day, 24 * 60).getTime() + bufferMs).toISOString();\r\n      const busy = await loadBusyIntervals({ supabase, gcal, rangeStartIso: rangeStart, rangeEndIso: rangeEnd });\r\n      const payload = {\r\n        slots: generateSingleSessionSlots({ nowMs, hostTz, rangesByDow, overridesByDate, busy, event }),\r\n        timezone: hostTz,\r\n      };\r\n      slotsCache.set(event.slug, { expiresAt: Date.now() + 15_000, payload });\r\n      return json(payload);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "status") {\r\n      const checkoutSessionId = String(body.checkout_session_id || "");\r\n      const token = String(body.booking_token || "");\r\n      if (!checkoutSessionId || !token) return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, 403);\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_single_session_status", {\r\n        _stripe_checkout_session_id: checkoutSessionId,\r\n        _booking_token_hash: await sha256Hex(token),\r\n      });\r\n      if (error || !data) return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, error?.code === "P0403" ? 403 : 404);\r\n      return json(data);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "hold") {\r\n      const name = String(body.name || "").trim().replace(/\\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 120);\r\n      const email = normalizeEmail(body.email);\r\n      const notes = String(body.notes || "").trim().slice(0, 2000);\r\n      const guests = normalizeGuests(body.guests, email);\r\n      const clientIdempotencyKey = String(body.client_idempotency_key || "").trim();\r\n      const startAt = new Date(String(body.start_at || ""));\r\n      if (!name || clientIdempotencyKey.length < 8 || !Number.isFinite(startAt.getTime())) return json({ error: "invalid_request" }, 400);\r\n      if (!await verifyTurnstile(String(body.turnstile_token || ""), ip)) return json({ error: "verification_failed" }, 403);\r\n      const event = await loadEvent(supabase, body.event);\r\n      const endAt = new Date(startAt.getTime() + event.lengthMinutes * 60_000);\r\n\r\n      // A replay must not conflict with its own DB hold or tentative Google\r\n      // event. New claims still take the live observation immediately before\r\n      // the authoritative SQL claim.\r\n      const { data: existingReplay, error: replayLookupError } = await supabase\r\n        .from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("id")\r\n        .eq("client_idempotency_key", clientIdempotencyKey)\r\n        .maybeSingle();\r\n      if (replayLookupError) throw replayLookupError;\r\n      if (!existingReplay) {\r\n        const liveBusy = await loadBusyIntervals({\r\n          supabase,\r\n          gcal,\r\n          rangeStartIso: startAt.toISOString(),\r\n          rangeEndIso: endAt.toISOString(),\r\n        });\r\n        if (liveBusy.some((interval) => interval.start < endAt.getTime() && interval.end > startAt.getTime())) {\r\n          return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const replayToken = String(body.booking_token || "");\r\n      const bookingToken = replayToken || generateCapabilityToken();\r\n      const bookingTokenHash = await sha256Hex(bookingToken);\r\n      const { data: claimed, error: claimError } = await supabase.rpc("claim_single_session_slot", {\r\n        _start_at: startAt.toISOString(),\r\n        _name: name,\r\n        _email: email,\r\n        _notes: notes || null,\r\n        _guests: guests,\r\n        _client_idempotency_key: clientIdempotencyKey,\r\n        _booking_token_hash: bookingTokenHash,\r\n        _ip_hash: ipHash,\r\n        _user_id: await optionalUserId(supabase, req),\r\n        _event_slug: event.slug,\r\n      });\r\n      if (claimError) {\r\n        // P0410 is a one-off link that was already used. It is a 409 like a\r\n        // taken slot, but it needs its own word: no other time will work either.\r\n        if (claimError.code === "P0410") return json({ error: "link_already_used" }, 409);\r\n        const status = claimError.code === "P0429" ? 429\r\n          : ["P0409", "P0425"].includes(claimError.code) ? 409\r\n          : claimError.code === "P0400" ? 400\r\n          : 500;\r\n        if (status === 409) return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n        return json({ error: status === 429 ? "rate_limited" : status === 400 ? "invalid_request" : "server_error" }, status);\r\n      }\r\n      let booking = Array.isArray(claimed) ? claimed[0] : claimed;\r\n      if (!booking) throw new Error("claim_returned_no_booking");\r\n      const encryptionSecret = requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY");\r\n      if (booking.status !== "draft") {\r\n        let responseBookingToken = booking.booking_token_hash === bookingTokenHash ? bookingToken : "";\r\n        if (!responseBookingToken && booking.checkout_request_fingerprint) {\r\n          const replayFingerprint = await decryptEnvelope<ReturnType<typeof buildCheckoutFingerprint>>(\r\n            booking.checkout_request_fingerprint,\r\n            encryptionSecret,\r\n          );\r\n          responseBookingToken = bookingTokenFromUrl(replayFingerprint.success_url) || "";\r\n        }\r\n        if (!responseBookingToken) return json({ error: "idempotency_token_required" }, 409);\r\n        return json({\r\n          booking: toPublicBooking(booking),\r\n          booking_token: responseBookingToken,\r\n          checkout_session_id: booking.stripe_checkout_session_id ?? null,\r\n          checkout_url: booking.stripe_checkout_url ?? null,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const targetCalendar = await calendarId(supabase);\r\n      let tentative: any;\r\n      try {\r\n        tentative = await adoptTentativeEvent(targetCalendar, booking.id, booking.start_at, booking.end_at);\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("set_single_session_tentative_event", {\r\n          _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n          _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n          _gcal_event_id: tentative.id,\r\n        });\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        booking = data ?? booking;\r\n      } catch (calendarError) {\r\n        await supabase.rpc("expire_single_session_draft", {\r\n          _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n          _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n          _reason: "tentative_calendar_failed",\r\n        });\r\n        await cleanupTentative(targetCalendar, booking.id, tentative?.id);\r\n        console.error("single-session tentative event failed", { bookingId: booking.id, error: calendarError instanceof Error ? calendarError.message : String(calendarError) });\r\n        return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      // A free event has no middle step. The slot is held and the tentative\r\n      // calendar event is up; there is no payment to wait for, so confirm now.\r\n      // finalize_single_session_free refuses on any event whose type says it\r\n      // requires payment, so this door cannot be walked through by a session.\r\n      if (!event.requiresPayment) {\r\n        const cancelToken = generateCapabilityToken();\r\n        const cancelPayload = await encryptEnvelope({\r\n          cancel_token: cancelToken,\r\n          cancel_url: `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/${event.slug}?cancel_token=${encodeURIComponent(cancelToken)}`,\r\n        }, encryptionSecret);\r\n        // Same last look the paid path takes on the webhook, and for the same\r\n        // reason: the tentative event we just wrote is our own, so it has to be\r\n        // excluded or every booking would look like a clash with itself.\r\n        const liveCalendarClear = await calendarClearFor(supabase, booking);\r\n        const { data: freeResult, error: freeError } = await supabase.rpc("finalize_single_session_free", {\r\n          _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n          _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n          _live_calendar_clear: liveCalendarClear,\r\n          _cancel_token_hash: await sha256Hex(cancelToken),\r\n          _cancel_payload_ciphertext: cancelPayload,\r\n        });\r\n        if (freeError) {\r\n          await cleanupTentative(targetCalendar, booking.id, booking.gcal_event_id);\r\n          const status = freeError.code === "P0409" ? 409 : freeError.code === "P0400" ? 400 : 500;\r\n          if (status === 409) return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n          return json({ error: status === 400 ? "invalid_request" : "server_error" }, status);\r\n        }\r\n        if (freeResult?.status !== "booked") {\r\n          await cleanupTentative(targetCalendar, booking.id, booking.gcal_event_id);\r\n          return slotUnavailable(event.slug);\r\n        }\r\n        const { data: confirmed } = await supabase\r\n          .from("single_session_bookings").select("*").eq("id", booking.id).maybeSingle();\r\n        return json({\r\n          booking: toPublicBooking(confirmed ?? booking),\r\n          booking_token: responseBookingTokenForFree(booking, bookingTokenHash, bookingToken),\r\n          checkout_session_id: null,\r\n          checkout_url: null,\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      let fingerprint: ReturnType<typeof buildCheckoutFingerprint>;\r\n      let responseBookingToken = booking.booking_token_hash === bookingTokenHash ? bookingToken : "";\r\n      if (booking.checkout_request_fingerprint) {\r\n        fingerprint = await decryptEnvelope(booking.checkout_request_fingerprint, encryptionSecret);\r\n        responseBookingToken ||= bookingTokenFromUrl(fingerprint.success_url) || "";\r\n      } else {\r\n        const tokenForUrl = responseBookingToken;\r\n        if (!tokenForUrl) throw new Error("idempotent_draft_missing_fingerprint");\r\n        const successUrl = `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/session?checkout_session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&booking_token=${encodeURIComponent(tokenForUrl)}`;\r\n        const cancelUrl = `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/session?cancelled=1&booking_token=${encodeURIComponent(tokenForUrl)}`;\r\n        fingerprint = buildCheckoutFingerprint({\r\n          bookingId: booking.id,\r\n          description: checkoutDescription(booking.start_at, String(body.timezone || SINGLE_SESSION.hostTimezone)),\r\n          email,\r\n          amountCents: event.priceCents,\r\n          currency: event.currency,\r\n          successUrl,\r\n          cancelUrl,\r\n          expiresAt: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + SINGLE_SESSION.checkoutMinutes * 60,\r\n        });\r\n        const encrypted = await encryptEnvelope(fingerprint, encryptionSecret);\r\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("persist_single_session_checkout_fingerprint", {\r\n          _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n          _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n          _fingerprint_ciphertext: encrypted,\r\n        });\r\n        if (error) throw error;\r\n        booking = data ?? { ...booking, checkout_request_fingerprint: encrypted };\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n      const session = await createCheckoutSession(stripeSecret, fingerprint, booking.id);\r\n      if (!session.id || !session.url || !session.expires_at) throw new Error("stripe_checkout_incomplete_response");\r\n      const { data: attached, error: attachError } = await supabase.rpc("attach_single_session_checkout", {\r\n        _booking_id: booking.id,\r\n        _expected_version: booking.lifecycle_version,\r\n        _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n        _stripe_checkout_url: session.url,\r\n        _stripe_expires_at: new Date(session.expires_at * 1000).toISOString(),\r\n      });\r\n      if (attachError) {\r\n        await expireCheckoutSession(stripeSecret, session.id).catch(() => undefined);\r\n        throw attachError;\r\n      }\r\n      return json({\r\n        booking: toPublicBooking(attached),\r\n        booking_token: responseBookingToken,\r\n        checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n        checkout_url: session.url,\r\n      });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "abandon") {\r\n      const checkoutSessionId = String(body.checkout_session_id || "");\r\n      const bookingToken = String(body.booking_token || "");\r\n      if (!checkoutSessionId || !bookingToken) return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, 403);\r\n      const bookingTokenHash = await sha256Hex(bookingToken);\r\n      const { data: current, error: statusError } = await supabase.rpc("get_single_session_status", {\r\n        _stripe_checkout_session_id: checkoutSessionId,\r\n        _booking_token_hash: bookingTokenHash,\r\n      });\r\n      if (statusError || !current) return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, 403);\r\n      if (current.status !== "checkout_open") return json(current);\r\n      try { await expireCheckoutSession(requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY"), checkoutSessionId); }\r\n      catch (error) {\r\n        console.error("single-session abandon stripe expiry failed", { checkoutSessionId, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n        return json({ error: "unable_to_abandon" }, 503);\r\n      }\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("abandon_single_session_booking", {\r\n        _booking_id: current.id,\r\n        _booking_token_hash: bookingTokenHash,\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) return json({ error: "unable_to_abandon" }, error.code === "P0403" ? 403 : 409);\r\n      return json(toPublicBooking(data));\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    if (action === "cancel") {\r\n      const token = String(body.cancel_token || "");\r\n      if (!token) return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, 403);\r\n      const cancelTokenHash = await sha256Hex(token);\r\n      if (body.confirm !== true) {\r\n        const { data: preview, error: previewError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n          .select("id,status,start_at,end_at,meet_url,stripe_checkout_session_id,cancel_token_expires_at")\r\n          .eq("cancel_token_hash", cancelTokenHash)\r\n          .gt("cancel_token_expires_at", new Date().toISOString())\r\n          .maybeSingle();\r\n        if (previewError) throw previewError;\r\n        if (!preview) return json({ error: "invalid_token" }, 403);\r\n        return json(toPublicBooking(preview));\r\n      }\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("cancel_single_session_booking", {\r\n        _cancel_token_hash: cancelTokenHash,\r\n      });\r\n      if (error) {\r\n        const status = error.code === "P0403" ? 403 : error.code === "P0409" ? 409 : 500;\r\n        return json({ error: status === 409 ? "cancellation_closed" : status === 403 ? "invalid_token" : "server_error" }, status);\r\n      }\r\n      return json(toPublicBooking(data));\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    return json({ error: "unknown_action" }, 400);\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "payload_too_large") return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);\r\n    // A slug that names no active event is not a fault in the server. It is a\r\n    // revoked, mistyped or retired link, and the page can only say so if it\r\n    // arrives as its own answer rather than as a 500 with the reason withheld.\r\n    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "unknown_event") return json({ error: "unknown_event" }, 404);\r\n    const detail = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n    console.error("single-session error", { error: detail });\r\n    // Students see nothing but "server_error". An admin, proven by their own\r\n    // JWT, gets the actual reason - otherwise a broken booking page is a\r\n    // guessing game from the outside.\r\n    if (await callerIsAdmin(req)) return json({ error: "server_error", detail }, 500);\r\n    return json({ error: "server_error" }, 500);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { createClient } from "npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2";\n\nimport { deleteEvent, getEvent, patchEvent, meetUrlFromEvent } from "../_shared/googleCalendar.ts";\nimport { decryptEnvelope } from "../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts";\nimport { createRefund, retrieveRefund } from "../_shared/stripe.ts";\nimport { sendAppEmail } from "../_shared/sendAppEmail.ts";\n\nconst WORKER_NAME = "single-session-outbox";\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 1024;\n\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Cache-Control": "no-store" } });\n}\n\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\n  return value;\n}\n\nasync function authorizeTicket(supabase: any, req: Request): Promise<boolean> {\n  const ticket = req.headers.get("x-worker-ticket") || "";\n  if (!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(ticket)) return false;\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("consume_booking_worker_ticket", {\n    _token: ticket,\n    _worker: WORKER_NAME,\n  });\n  if (error) {\n    console.error("worker ticket check failed", { code: error.code });\n    return false;\n  }\n  return data === true;\n}\n\nfunction authorize(req: Request): boolean {\n  const expected = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET") ?? "";\n  if (!expected) return false;\n  const supplied = req.headers.get("x-worker-secret") || "";\n  if (expected.length !== supplied.length) return false;\n  let difference = 0;\n  for (let index = 0; index < expected.length; index++) difference |= expected.charCodeAt(index) ^ supplied.charCodeAt(index);\n  return difference === 0;\n}\n\nasync function heartbeat(supabase: any, phase: "start" | "success" | "error", error?: string, metadata: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\n  const { error: heartbeatError } = await supabase.rpc("record_booking_worker_heartbeat", {\n    _worker_name: WORKER_NAME,\n    _phase: phase,\n    _error: error ?? null,\n    _metadata: metadata,\n  });\n  if (heartbeatError) throw heartbeatError;\n}\n\nfunction whenText(startAt: string): string {\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {\n    timeZone: "America/Toronto",\n    weekday: "long",\n    month: "long",\n    day: "numeric",\n    hour: "numeric",\n    minute: "2-digit",\n    timeZoneName: "short",\n  }).format(new Date(startAt));\n}\n\nasync function sendEmail(job: any, booking: any, payload: any) {\n  const admin = job.recipient === "admin";\n  const recipientEmail = admin ? requiredEnv("BOOKING_ADMIN_EMAIL") : booking.email;\n  if (!recipientEmail) throw new Error("email_recipient_missing");\n  let templateName = "booking-confirmation";\n  let templateData: Record<string, unknown> = {\n    recipientRole: admin ? "admin" : "student",\n    studentName: booking.name,\n    studentEmail: booking.email,\n    whenText: whenText(booking.start_at),\n    durationMinutes: 70,\n    meetUrl: booking.meet_url,\n    studentNote: admin ? booking.notes : "",\n    fileNames: [],\n    cancelUrl: admin ? undefined : payload?.cancel_url,\n    calendarPending: !booking.meet_url,\n  };\n  if (["email_cancellation", "email_refund_apology"].includes(job.action)) {\n    templateName = "single-session-update";\n    templateData = {\n      kind: job.action === "email_refund_apology" ? "refund" : "cancellation",\n      recipientRole: admin ? "admin" : "booker",\n      studentName: booking.name,\n      whenText: whenText(booking.start_at),\n    };\n  }\n  await sendAppEmail(templateName, recipientEmail, {\n    idempotencyKey: job.idempotency_key,\n    templateData,\n  });\n}\n\nasync function complete(supabase: any, job: any, options: { providerStatus?: string; result?: any; meetUrl?: string } = {}) {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("complete_single_session_outbox", {\n    _job_id: job.id,\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\n    _provider_status: options.providerStatus ?? null,\n    _result: options.result ?? {},\n    _meet_url: options.meetUrl ?? null,\n  });\n  if (error) throw error;\n  return data;\n}\n\nasync function fail(supabase: any, job: any, error: unknown, providerStatus?: string, confirmedFailure = false) {\n  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n  const { error: failureError } = await supabase.rpc("fail_single_session_outbox", {\n    _job_id: job.id,\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\n    _error: message,\n    _provider_status: providerStatus ?? null,\n    _confirmed_failure: confirmedFailure,\n  });\n  if (failureError && failureError.code !== "P0409") throw failureError;\n  return message;\n}\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\n  try {\n    const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\n    if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);\n    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\n    // Two accepted callers: a worker holding BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET, and the\n    // scheduled job, which presents a single-use ticket only a service-role\n    // reader could have minted. No shared secret lives in SQL or cron metadata.\n    if (!authorize(req) && !(await authorizeTicket(supabase, req))) {\n      return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);\n    }\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "start");\n    const workerId = `${WORKER_NAME}:${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n    const { data: jobs, error: claimError } = await supabase.rpc("claim_single_session_outbox", {\n      _worker_id: workerId,\n      _actions: null,\n      _limit: 20,\n      _lease_seconds: 180,\n    });\n    if (claimError) throw claimError;\n\n    const results: { id: string; action: string; outcome: string }[] = [];\n    for (const job of jobs ?? []) {\n      const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\n        .select("*").eq("id", job.booking_id).maybeSingle();\n      if (bookingError || !booking) {\n        await fail(supabase, job, bookingError ?? new Error("booking_not_found"));\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (booking.status !== job.precondition_status || booking.lifecycle_version !== job.precondition_lifecycle_version) {\n        await fail(supabase, job, new Error("lifecycle_precondition_changed"));\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "superseded" });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      try {\n        if (job.action === "gcal_promote") {\n          if (!booking.gcal_event_id) throw new Error("tentative_event_missing");\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\n          const targetCalendar = state?.calendar_id || "primary";\n          const attendees = [booking.email, ...((booking.guests ?? []) as string[])].filter(Boolean).map((email) => ({ email }));\n          let event = await patchEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id, {\n            summary: `${String(booking.name || "Student").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0]} session`,\n            description: booking.notes ? `Booked via LSAT U.\\n\\nNotes from Booker:\\n${booking.notes}` : "Booked via LSAT U.",\n            attendees,\n            visibility: "default",\n            conferenceData: { createRequest: { requestId: booking.id, conferenceSolutionKey: { type: "hangoutsMeet" } } },\n            extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_single_session_booking_id: booking.id, lsatu_hold: "false" } },\n          }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: "all" });\n          let meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\n          if (!meetUrl) {\n            event = await getEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id);\n            meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\n          }\n          if (!meetUrl) throw new Error("meet_url_pending");\n          const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({\n            google_event_id: event.id,\n            calendar_id: targetCalendar,\n            title: event.summary,\n            description: event.description,\n            start_at: booking.start_at,\n            end_at: booking.end_at,\n            all_day: false,\n            status: event.status ?? "confirmed",\n            html_link: event.htmlLink ?? null,\n            meet_url: meetUrl,\n            source: "single_session",\n            etag: event.etag ?? null,\n            last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n            deleted_at: null,\n          }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" });\n          if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\n          const completion = await complete(supabase, job, { meetUrl, result: { google_event_id: event.id } });\n          if (completion?.compensate) {\n            await deleteEvent(targetCalendar, event.id, { sendUpdates: "all" });\n            await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({ status: "cancelled", deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("google_event_id", event.id);\n          }\n        } else if (job.action === "gcal_delete") {\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\n          const eventId = booking.gcal_event_id;\n          if (eventId) {\n            await deleteEvent(state?.calendar_id || "primary", eventId, { sendUpdates: booking.status === "cancelled" ? "all" : "none" });\n            const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({\n              status: "cancelled",\n              deleted_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n              last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n            }).eq("google_event_id", eventId);\n            if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\n          }\n          await complete(supabase, job, { result: { google_event_id: eventId ?? null } });\n        } else if (job.action === "stripe_refund") {\n          if (!booking.stripe_payment_intent_id) throw new Error("payment_intent_missing");\n          const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\n          const refund = booking.stripe_refund_id\n            ? await retrieveRefund(stripeSecret, booking.stripe_refund_id)\n            : await createRefund(stripeSecret, {\n              payment_intent: booking.stripe_payment_intent_id,\n              amount: 4500,\n              metadata: { booking_id: booking.id, product: "single_session" },\n            }, job.idempotency_key);\n          if (refund.status === "succeeded") {\n            await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: refund.status, result: { refund_id: refund.id } });\n          } else if (["failed", "canceled"].includes(String(refund.status))) {\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_confirmed_failed"), String(refund.status), true);\n          } else {\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_pending"), String(refund.status), false);\n          }\n        } else if (job.action.startsWith("email_")) {\n          const payload = job.payload_ciphertext\n            ? await decryptEnvelope(job.payload_ciphertext, requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY"))\n            : {};\n          await sendEmail(job, booking, payload);\n          await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: "accepted" });\n        } else {\n          throw new Error("unknown_outbox_action");\n        }\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "processed" });\n      } catch (error) {\n        await fail(supabase, job, error);\n        console.error("single-session outbox job failed", { bookingId: booking.id, jobId: job.id, action: job.action, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\n      }\n    }\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "success", undefined, { processed: results.length });\n    return json({ processed: results.length, results });\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    try {\n      const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\n      await heartbeat(supabase, "error", message);\n    } catch (_) { /* fail-closed response is the durable alert when heartbeat is unavailable */ }\n    console.error("single-session-outbox error", { error: message });\n    return json({ error: "worker_failed" }, 500);\n  }\n});\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 7.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- What a bookable event IS, as data instead of as constants.\n--\n-- The single session\'s shape -- 70 minutes, 24 hours\' notice, no buffers, a six\n-- business day window, $45 -- is currently written into the edge function AND\n-- into three SQL functions that enforce the slot policy inside the booking\n-- transaction. That is why there can only ever be one kind of session: a\n-- consultation is 30 minutes with 30-minute buffers and a five day window, and\n-- there is nowhere to say so.\n--\n-- This migration only creates the vocabulary and seeds it with the values that\n-- are live today. Nothing reads it yet, so this changes no behavior. The SQL\n-- policy functions are switched over to it next, one at a time.\n--\n-- Every number here was read from Joshua\'s Cal.com account on 2026-08-30, which\n-- stays the reference for what these events are supposed to be.\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_event_types (\n  slug text PRIMARY KEY,\n  label text NOT NULL,\n  length_minutes int NOT NULL CHECK (length_minutes > 0),\n  -- Null means "start a slot every length_minutes", which is what Cal.com does\n  -- when no explicit interval is set. The single session\'s back-to-back 70\n  -- minute chain (1:00, 2:10, 3:20) is this rule, not a quirk.\n  slot_interval_minutes int CHECK (slot_interval_minutes IS NULL OR slot_interval_minutes > 0),\n  minimum_notice_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440 CHECK (minimum_notice_minutes >= 0),\n  buffer_before_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_before_minutes >= 0),\n  buffer_after_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_after_minutes >= 0),\n  -- Business days size the window; days inside it are offered whenever the\n  -- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.\n  window_business_days int NOT NULL DEFAULT 6 CHECK (window_business_days > 0),\n  price_cents int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\n  currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'usd\',\n  requires_payment boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nINSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (\n  slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,\n  buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,\n  price_cents, currency, requires_payment\n) VALUES\n  (\'single-session\', \'LSAT Session\', 70, NULL, 1440, 0,  0,  6, 4500, \'usd\', true),\n  (\'consultation\',   \'Consultation\', 30, NULL, 1440, 30, 30, 5,    0, \'usd\', false)\nON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;\n\n-- One student, one price. Joshua already runs this by hand on Cal.com -- there\n-- are twelve event types on that account and most of them exist only because a\n-- particular person pays a particular rate. Keyed by email because the booker\n-- is not necessarily signed in when they book.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_price_overrides (\n  email text NOT NULL,\n  event_slug text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  price_cents int NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\n  note text,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  PRIMARY KEY (email, event_slug)\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_price_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\n-- Students never read these directly: the booking page gets everything it needs\n-- from the edge function, which runs as service_role.\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking event types"\n  ON public.booking_event_types FOR ALL TO authenticated\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking price overrides"\n  ON public.booking_price_overrides FOR ALL TO authenticated\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_event_types TO authenticated;\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_price_overrides TO authenticated;\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_event_types TO service_role;\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_price_overrides TO service_role;\n\n-- The price a given booker pays for a given event: their override, else the\n-- event\'s own price. One definition, so the checkout and the payment\n-- verification can never disagree about what was owed.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(_event_slug text, _email text)\nRETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    (SELECT o.price_cents FROM public.booking_price_overrides o\n      WHERE o.event_slug = _event_slug AND lower(o.email) = lower(COALESCE(_email, \'\'))),\n    (SELECT t.price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _event_slug)\n  );\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) TO service_role;\n\r\n'}]
