WHEN OTHERS THEN\r\n  RETURN false;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(_start_at timestamptz, _name text, _email text, _notes text, _guests jsonb, _client_idempotency_key text, _booking_token_hash text, _ip_hash text, _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'::text)\r\nRETURNS single_session_bookings\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path TO 'public'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _end_at timestamptz;\r\n  _email_normalized text := lower(trim(_email));\r\n  _guests_normalized jsonb;\r\n  _expired record;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\r\n  _length := COALESCE(_event.length_minutes, 70);\r\n  _end_at := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _length);\r\n\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN\r\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_booking.start_at));\r\n    SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking.id FOR UPDATE;\r\n    RETURN _booking;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at, now(), _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_unavailable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0425';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF NULLIF(trim(_name), '') IS NULL OR length(trim(_name)) > 200 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_name' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF _email_normalized !~ '^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$'\r\n     OR length(_email_normalized) > 320 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_email' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF length(COALESCE(_notes, '')) > 4000 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'notes_too_long' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF length(COALESCE(_client_idempotency_key, '')) NOT BETWEEN 8 AND 200\r\n     OR length(COALESCE(_booking_token_hash, '')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256\r\n     OR length(COALESCE(_ip_hash, '')) NOT BETWEEN 16 AND 256 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_capability' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF jsonb_typeof(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) <> 'array'\r\n     OR jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) > 10 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_guests' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(g.email) ORDER BY g.email), '[]'::jsonb)\r\n  INTO _guests_normalized\r\n  FROM (\r\n    SELECT DISTINCT lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) AS email\r\n    FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb))\r\n    WHERE jsonb_typeof(value) = 'string'\r\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) <> _email_normalized\r\n      AND lower(trim(value #>> '{}')) ~ '^[^@[:space:]]+@[^@[:space:]]+[.][^@[:space:]]+$'\r\n  ) g;\r\n  IF jsonb_array_length(_guests_normalized) <> jsonb_array_length(COALESCE(_guests, '[]'::jsonb)) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_or_duplicate_guests' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_start_at));\r\n\r\n  FOR _expired IN\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      SET status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n          terminal_reason = 'authoritative_expiry'\r\n    WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\r\n      AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) <= now()\r\n      AND tstzrange(b.start_at, b.end_at, '[)') && tstzrange(_start_at, _end_at, '[)')\r\n    RETURNING b.id, b.lifecycle_version, b.gcal_event_id\r\n  LOOP\r\n    IF _expired.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(\r\n        _expired.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending', 'expired',\r\n        _expired.lifecycle_version, NULL\r\n      );\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\r\n        AND b.ip_hash = _ip_hash) >= 3 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_ip' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()\r\n        AND lower(b.email) = _email_normalized) >= 3 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_email' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings b\r\n      WHERE b.status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open')\r\n        AND COALESCE(b.stripe_expires_at, b.draft_expires_at) > now()) >= 250 THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hold_cap_global' USING ERRCODE = 'P0429';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT public.booking_slot_is_clear(\r\n    _start_at, _end_at,\r\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0)\r\n  ) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  INSERT INTO public.single_session_bookings (\r\n    start_at, end_at, name, email, notes, guests, client_idempotency_key,\r\n    draft_expires_at, user_id, booking_token_hash, booking_token_expires_at, ip_hash,\r\n    event_slug\r\n  ) VALUES (\r\n    _start_at, _end_at, trim(_name), _email_normalized, NULLIF(trim(_notes), ''),\r\n    _guests_normalized, _client_idempotency_key, now() + interval '5 minutes',\r\n    _user_id, _booking_token_hash, now() + interval '24 hours', _ip_hash,\r\n    _event_slug\r\n  ) RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n\r\n  RETURN _booking;\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN exclusion_violation OR unique_violation THEN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\r\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot_conflict' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_free(_booking_id uuid, _expected_version integer, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\r\nRETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path TO 'public'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _can_book boolean := false;\r\n  _new_version integer;\r\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\r\n  IF _event.requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM false THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status = 'booked' THEN\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\r\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status <> 'draft' OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _expected_version THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'stale_booking' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\r\n    _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\r\n    COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\r\n    _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  IF NOT _can_book THEN\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n      status = 'expired', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n      terminal_reason = 'slot_taken_before_confirm'\r\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n    IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\r\n        'expired', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\r\n    END IF;\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\r\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n    status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n    amount_cents = 0, currency = 'usd',\r\n    cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\r\n    terminal_reason = NULL\r\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n  _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\r\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\r\n    'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\r\n  PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\r\n    'booked', _new_version, NULL);\r\n\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\r\n    'lifecycle_version', _new_version, 'was_existing', false);\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(_booking_id uuid, _provider_event_id text, _stripe_checkout_session_id text, _stripe_payment_intent_id text, _amount_cents integer, _currency text, _livemode boolean, _live_calendar_clear boolean, _cancel_token_hash text, _cancel_payload_ciphertext text)\r\nRETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path TO 'public'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\r\n  _can_book boolean := false;\r\n  _new_version integer;\r\n  _slot_start timestamptz;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, '') IS NULL\r\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL\r\n     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, '') IS NULL\r\n     OR _amount_cents <> 4500 OR lower(COALESCE(_currency, '')) <> 'usd'\r\n     OR _livemode IS NULL THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_payment_facts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\r\n\r\n  SELECT single_session_booking_id INTO _claimed_booking_id\r\n  FROM public.booking_commerce_events\r\n  WHERE provider = 'stripe' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id\r\n    AND event_type = 'single_session_paid';\r\n  IF FOUND AND _claimed_booking_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking_id THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'provider_event_already_claimed' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.stripe_checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_checkout_session_id THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'checkout_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status IN ('booked', 'cancelled', 'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed') THEN\r\n    IF _booking.stripe_payment_intent_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_payment_intent_id\r\n       OR _booking.amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _amount_cents\r\n       OR lower(_booking.currency) IS DISTINCT FROM lower(_currency)\r\n       OR _booking.livemode IS DISTINCT FROM _livemode THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_facts_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n    END IF;\r\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\r\n      provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\r\n      payload, single_session_booking_id\r\n    ) VALUES (\r\n      'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\r\n      _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\r\n      jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\r\n      _booking_id\r\n    ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\r\n      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _booking.status NOT IN ('checkout_open', 'expired', 'abandoned') THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_finalizable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\r\n    provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\r\n    payload, single_session_booking_id\r\n  ) VALUES (\r\n    'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', 'single-session-native',\r\n    _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\r\n    jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\r\n    _booking_id\r\n  ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\r\n\r\n  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\r\n    AND (_booking.status = 'checkout_open'\r\n      OR (_booking.status = 'expired' AND _booking.status_changed_at >= now() - interval '10 minutes'))\r\n    AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\r\n      _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\r\n      COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\r\n      _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\r\n    );\r\n\r\n  IF _can_book THEN\r\n    IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\r\n    END IF;\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n      status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\r\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\r\n      cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\r\n      terminal_reason = NULL\r\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n\r\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\r\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\r\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\r\n      'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\r\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\r\n      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\r\n  ELSE\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n      status = 'refund_pending', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\r\n      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\r\n      terminal_reason = 'paid_slot_unavailable'\r\n    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = 'superseded', updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state IN ('blocked', 'pending', 'failed')\r\n        AND action IN ('gcal_promote', 'email_booking_confirmation', 'email_admin_notification');\r\n"}]

EXCEPTION \'invalid_booking_token\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0403\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF _booking.status = \'abandoned\' THEN RETURN _booking; END IF;\r\n  IF _booking.status <> \'checkout_open\' THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'abandon_not_allowed\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n    status = \'abandoned\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n    terminal_reason = \'booker_abandoned\'\r\n  WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n  IF _booking.gcal_event_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'gcal_delete\', \'system\', \'pending\',\r\n      _booking.status, _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\r\n  END IF;\r\n  RETURN _booking;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_single_session_status(\r\n  _stripe_checkout_session_id text,\r\n  _booking_token_hash text\r\n) RETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE stripe_checkout_session_id = _stripe_checkout_session_id\r\n    AND booking_token_hash = _booking_token_hash;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _booking.booking_token_expires_at <= now() THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'invalid_booking_token\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0403\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  RETURN jsonb_strip_nulls(jsonb_build_object(\r\n    \'id\', _booking.id,\r\n    \'booking_id\', _booking.id,\r\n    \'status\', _booking.status,\r\n    \'start_at\', _booking.start_at,\r\n    \'end_at\', _booking.end_at,\r\n    \'meet_url\', CASE WHEN _booking.status = \'booked\' THEN _booking.meet_url ELSE NULL END,\r\n    \'calendar_pending\', (_booking.status = \'booked\' AND _booking.meet_url IS NULL)\r\n  ));\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(\r\n  _booking_id uuid,\r\n  _provider_event_id text,\r\n  _stripe_checkout_session_id text,\r\n  _stripe_payment_intent_id text,\r\n  _amount_cents integer,\r\n  _currency text,\r\n  _livemode boolean,\r\n  _live_calendar_clear boolean,\r\n  _cancel_token_hash text,\r\n  _cancel_payload_ciphertext text\r\n  RETURN QUERY\r\n  WITH candidates AS (\r\n    SELECT o.id\r\n    FROM public.single_session_outbox o\r\n    WHERE (o.state = \'pending\'\r\n        OR (o.state = \'leased\' AND o.lease_until <= now()))\r\n      AND o.next_retry_at <= now()\r\n      AND o.attempts < o.max_attempts\r\n      AND (_actions IS NULL OR o.action = ANY(_actions))\r\n    ORDER BY o.next_retry_at, o.created_at\r\n    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED\r\n    LIMIT _limit\r\n  )\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_outbox o SET\r\n    state = \'leased\', attempts = o.attempts + 1,\r\n    lease_worker_id = _worker_id, lease_token = gen_random_uuid(),\r\n    lease_until = now() + make_interval(secs => _lease_seconds), updated_at = now()\r\n  FROM candidates c WHERE o.id = c.id\r\n  RETURNING o.*;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.complete_single_session_outbox(\r\n  _job_id uuid,\r\n  _lease_token uuid,\r\n  _provider_status text DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _result jsonb DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\r\n  _meet_url text DEFAULT NULL\r\n) RETURNS jsonb\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _job public.single_session_outbox%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _compensate boolean := false;\r\n  _refund_id text;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _job FROM public.single_session_outbox WHERE id = _job_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'outbox_job_not_found\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0404\'; END IF;\r\n  IF _job.state <> \'leased\' OR _job.lease_token IS DISTINCT FROM _lease_token\r\n     OR _job.lease_until <= now() THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'outbox_lease_lost\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0409\';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(\'single-session-side-effect:\' || _job.booking_id::text, 0));\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _job.booking_id FOR UPDATE;\r\n  IF _booking.status <> _job.precondition_status\r\n     OR _booking.lifecycle_version <> _job.precondition_lifecycle_version THEN\r\n    _compensate := _job.action = \'gcal_promote\';\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET\r\n      state = \'superseded\', lease_worker_id = NULL, lease_token = NULL,\r\n      lease_until = NULL, last_error = \'lifecycle changed during side effect\', updated_at = now()\r\n    WHERE id = _job_id;\r\n    RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'applied\', false, \'compensate\', _compensate);\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _job.action = \'gcal_promote\' THEN\r\n    IF NULLIF(_meet_url, \'\') IS NULL THEN\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION \'meet_url_required\' USING ERRCODE = \'P0400\';\r\n    END IF;\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings SET meet_url = _meet_url WHERE id = _booking.id;\r\n    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = \'pending\', next_retry_at = now(), updated_at = now()\r\n    WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state = \'blocked\'\r\n      AND action IN (\'email_booking_confirmation\', \'email_admin_notification\')\r\n      AND precondition_status = _booking.status\r\n      AND precondition_lifecycle_version = _booking.lifecycle_version;\r\n  ELSIF _job.action = \'stripe_refund\' THEN\r\n    _refund_id := NULLIF(_result->>\'refund_id\', \'\');\r\n    IF _refund_id IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.single_session_bookings SET\r\n        stripe_refund_id = COALESCE(stripe_refund_id, _refund_id),\r\n        stripe_refund_status = COALESCE(_provider_status, stripe_refund_status)\r\n      WHERE id = _booking.id;\r\n    END IF;\r\n    IF lower(COALESCE(_provider_status, \'\')) = \'succeeded\' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\r\n        status = \'refunded\', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\r\n        stripe_refund_status = \'succeeded\'\r\n      WHERE b.id = _booking.id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\r\n      PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, \'email_refund_apology\', \'booker\', \'pending\',\r\n        \'refunded\', _booking.lifecycle_version, NULL);\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET\r\n    state = \'succeeded\', provider_status = _provider_status,\r\n    result = COALESCE(_result, \'{}\'::jsonb), payload_ciphertext = NULL,\r\n    sensitive_payload_purged_at = CASE WHEN payload_ciphertext IS NULL\r\n      THEN sensitive_payload_purged_at ELSE now() END,\r\n    lease_worker_id = NULL, lease_token = NULL, lease_until = NULL,\r\n    completed_at = now(), updated_at = now()\r\n  WHERE id = _job_id;\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\'applied\', true, \'compensate\', false);\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.fail_single_session_outbox(\r\n  _job_id uuid,\r\n  _lease_token uuid,\r\n  _error text,\r\n  _provider_status text DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _confirmed_failure boolean DEFAULT false\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- Native Single Session booking: durable Hold/booking lifecycle and side-effect outbox.\r\n-- The public edge function is the only public interface; both tables are service-role only.\r\n\r\nCREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_bookings (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  start_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  name text,\r\n  email text,\r\n  notes text,\r\n  guests jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'[]\'::jsonb,\r\n  client_idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  draft_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  checkout_request_fingerprint text,\r\n  checkout_fingerprint_disposition text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'none\',\r\n  stripe_checkout_session_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_checkout_url text,\r\n  stripe_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_payment_intent_id text,\r\n  amount_cents integer,\r\n  currency text,\r\n  livemode boolean,\r\n  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\r\n  booking_token_hash text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  booking_token_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  cancel_token_hash text UNIQUE,\r\n  cancel_token_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_refund_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_refund_status text,\r\n  gcal_event_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  meet_url text,\r\n  lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'draft\',\r\n  status_changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  terminal_reason text,\r\n  ip_hash text,\r\n  sweep_worker_id text,\r\n  sweep_lease_token uuid,\r\n  sweep_lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  sweep_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_duration_chk CHECK (end_at = start_at + interval \'70 minutes\'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_guests_chk CHECK (jsonb_typeof(guests) = \'array\' AND jsonb_array_length(guests) <= 10),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_lifecycle_version_chk CHECK (lifecycle_version >= 0),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_chk CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents = 4500),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_currency_chk CHECK (currency IS NULL OR lower(currency) = \'usd\'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_status_chk CHECK (status IN (\r\n    \'draft\', \'checkout_open\', \'booked\', \'cancelled\', \'expired\', \'abandoned\',\r\n    \'refund_pending\', \'refunded\', \'refund_failed\'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_fingerprint_disposition_chk CHECK (checkout_fingerprint_disposition IN (\r\n    \'none\', \'persisted\', \'attached\', \'stripe_expired\', \'stripe_absent\'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_checkout_expiry_chk CHECK (\r\n    (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NOT NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NOT NULL)\r\n  ),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_cancel_token_chk CHECK (\r\n    (cancel_token_hash IS NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (cancel_token_hash IS NOT NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at = start_at)\r\n  )\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_no_occupying_overlap\r\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (tstzrange(start_at, end_at, \'[)\') WITH &&)\r\n  WHERE (status IN (\'draft\', \'checkout_open\', \'booked\'));\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_start_at_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (start_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_status_expiry_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (status, stripe_expires_at, draft_expires_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_email_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (lower(email)) WHERE email IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_user_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (user_id, start_at) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_sweep_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (sweep_lease_until, status, updated_at);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_outbox (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  booking_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.single_session_bookings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\r\n  action text NOT NULL,\r\n  recipient text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'system\',\r\n  state text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'pending\',\r\n  attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  max_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 8,\r\n  lease_worker_id text,\r\n  lease_token uuid,\r\n  lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  next_retry_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  provider_status text,\r\n  last_error text,\r\n  idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  precondition_status text NOT NULL,\r\n  precondition_lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL,\r\n  payload_ciphertext text,\r\n  result jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\r\n  sensitive_payload_purged_at timestamptz,\r\n  completed_at timestamptz,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_outbox_action_chk CHECK (action IN (\r\n    \'gcal_promote\', \'gcal_delete\', \'email_booking_confirmation\',\r\n    \'email_admin_notification\', \'email_cancellation\',\r\n    \'email_refund_apology\', \'stripe_refund\'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_outbox_state_chk CHECK (state IN (\r\n    \'blocked\', \'pending\', \'leased\', \'succeeded\', \'failed\', \'superseded\'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_outbox_attempts_chk CHECK (\r\n    attempts >= 0 AND max_attempts > 0 AND attempts <= max_attempts\r\n  ),\r\n  UNIQUE (booking_id, action, recipient)\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_outbox_claim_idx\r\n      _idempotency_key, _student_name, _student_email, \'pending\', true\r\n    ) RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\r\n  EXCEPTION WHEN unique_violation THEN\r\n    SELECT * INTO _existing FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions\r\n      WHERE booking_pass_id = _pass_id AND idempotency_key = _idempotency_key;\r\n    redemption_id := _existing.id; start_at := _existing.start_at; end_at := _existing.end_at;\r\n    used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\r\n    status := _pass.status; was_existing := true; RETURN NEXT; RETURN;\r\n  END;\r\n  UPDATE public.booking_passes bp SET\r\n    used_credits = bp.used_credits + 1,\r\n    status = CASE WHEN bp.used_credits + 1 >= bp.total_credits THEN \'exhausted\' ELSE \'active\' END\r\n  WHERE bp.id = _pass_id RETURNING * INTO _pass;\r\n  redemption_id := _new_id; start_at := _start_at; end_at := _end_at;\r\n  used_credits := _pass.used_credits; total_credits := _pass.total_credits;\r\n  status := _pass.status; was_existing := false; RETURN NEXT;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- 6. single-session policy\r\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types\r\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS window_calendar_days integer;\r\n\r\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types\r\nSET buffer_before_minutes = 10, buffer_after_minutes = 10,\r\n    slot_interval_minutes = 80, window_calendar_days = 8, updated_at = now()\r\nWHERE slug = \'single-session\';\r\nUPDATE public.booking_event_types\r\nSET window_calendar_days = 8, updated_at = now()\r\nWHERE slug = \'consultation\';\r\n\r\n-- Window end for an event: calendar days when set (Cal.com "calendarDays",\r\n-- rolling), else the business-day count it always had.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'::text)\r\nRETURNS date\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _day date := (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date;\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _target integer;\r\n  _business_days integer := 0;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug;\r\n  IF FOUND AND _event.window_calendar_days IS NOT NULL THEN\r\n    RETURN _day + _event.window_calendar_days;\r\n  END IF;\r\n  _target := COALESCE(_event.window_business_days, 6);\r\n  WHILE _business_days < _target LOOP\r\n    IF extract(isodow FROM _day) BETWEEN 1 AND 5 THEN\r\n      _business_days := _business_days + 1;\r\n    END IF;\r\n    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n  RETURN _day;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$;\r\n\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at timestamptz, _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT \'single-session\'::text)\r\nRETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE\r\nSET search_path TO \'public\'\r\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    \'America/Toronto\'\r\n  );\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _pitch integer;\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 5.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '    if (currency !== price.currency) throw new Error("mixed_payment_link_currency");\r\n    amount += price.unit_amount * item.quantity;\r\n  }\r\n  return { amount, currency };\r\n}\r\n\r\n// Server-to-server nudge so a paid booking\'s calendar promotion and emails run\r\n// immediately instead of waiting for the scheduled worker. The worker secret is\r\n// read from the function environment and never logged or returned. Failures are\r\n// swallowed: the every-minute scheduled worker is the retry safety net, and a\r\n// paid booking must never be undone by a missed nudge.\r\nasync function kickOutboxWorker(): Promise<void> {\r\n  const secret = Deno.env.get("BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET");\r\n  if (!secret) {\r\n    console.error("outbox nudge skipped: worker secret not configured");\r\n    return;\r\n  }\r\n  try {\r\n    const res = await fetch(`${requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL")}/functions/v1/single-session-outbox`, {\r\n      method: "POST",\r\n      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "x-worker-secret": secret },\r\n      body: "{}",\r\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),\r\n    });\r\n    if (!res.ok) console.error("outbox nudge non-2xx", { status: res.status });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    console.error("outbox nudge failed", { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function handleStripe(supabase: any, raw: string, signature: string | null) {\r\n  const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n  if (!signature) return json({ error: "invalid_signature" }, 400);\r\n  const stripe = new Stripe(stripeSecret, {\r\n    apiVersion: "2025-06-30.basil",\r\n    httpClient: Stripe.createFetchHttpClient(),\r\n      console.log("access grant", { event: event.id, ...grant });\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      // Access is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to open\r\n      // it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was already recorded.\r\n      console.error("access grant failed", { event: event.id, err });\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (session.metadata?.product === "single_session") {\r\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || "");\r\n    if (!bookingId) return json({ error: "invalid_product" }, 400);\r\n    if (!session.id || typeof session.payment_intent !== "string") return json({ error: "missing_payment_facts" }, 400);\r\n    const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n      .select("id,status,start_at,end_at,gcal_event_id,event_slug")\r\n      .eq("id", bookingId).maybeSingle();\r\n    if (bookingError) throw bookingError;\r\n    if (!booking) return json({ error: "unknown_booking" }, 400);\r\n    const { data: bookingEvent, error: eventError } = await supabase.from("booking_event_types")\r\n      .select("price_cents,currency")\r\n      .eq("slug", booking.event_slug).maybeSingle();\r\n    if (eventError) throw eventError;\r\n    if (!bookingEvent) return json({ error: "unknown_event" }, 400);\r\n    const validation = validateSingleSessionCheckout(\r\n      session,\r\n      expectedLivemode(),\r\n      Number(bookingEvent.price_cents),\r\n      String(bookingEvent.currency),\r\n    );\r\n    if (!validation.ok) return json({ error: validation.error }, 400);\r\n\r\n    const cancelToken = generateCapabilityToken();\r\n    const encryptedCancelPayload = await encryptEnvelope({\r\n      cancel_token: cancelToken,\r\n      cancel_url: `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/session?cancel_token=${encodeURIComponent(cancelToken)}`,\r\n    }, requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY"));\r\n    const { data: result, error } = await supabase.rpc("finalize_single_session_payment", {\r\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\r\n      _provider_event_id: event.id,\r\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\r\n      _stripe_payment_intent_id: session.payment_intent,\r\n      _amount_cents: session.amount_total,\r\n      _currency: session.currency,\r\n      _livemode: session.livemode,\r\n      _live_calendar_clear: await liveCalendarClear(supabase, booking),\r\n      _cancel_token_hash: await sha256Hex(cancelToken),\r\n      _cancel_payload_ciphertext: encryptedCancelPayload,\r\n    });\r\n    if (error) throw error;\r\n    // Keep the Stripe response fast: the nudge runs after the response is sent\r\n    // when the runtime supports it, and is otherwise best-effort.\r\n    const nudge = kickOutboxWorker();\r\n    const waitUntil = (globalThis as any)?.EdgeRuntime?.waitUntil;\r\n    if (typeof waitUntil === "function") waitUntil.call((globalThis as any).EdgeRuntime, nudge);\r\n    else void nudge;\r\n    return json({ received: true, booking_id: result?.booking_id, status: result?.status });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // Existing package branch, now protected by immutable payment validation.\r\n  if (session.mode !== "payment" || session.payment_status !== "paid") return json({ error: "payment_not_complete" }, 400);\r\n  if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: "livemode_mismatch" }, 400);\r\n  const paymentLink = typeof session.payment_link === "string" ? session.payment_link : session.payment_link?.id;\r\n'}]

## assistant

The current database still has two legacy assumptions that explain the $1 failure risk: payment finalization checks exactly 4,500 cents, and the booking row does not retain the customer’s selected timezone or quoted price. I’m replacing those with booking-specific facts while preserving the existing service-role-only RPC boundary.

## Tool: exec

const patch = "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-booking-confirmation\\supabase\\migrations\\20260915090000_booking_confirmation_details.sql\n+-- Persist the facts shown after checkout and remove the legacy $45-only\n+-- assumption from paid booking finalization.\n+\n+ALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n+  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS booker_timezone text,\n+  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS quoted_amount_cents integer,\n+  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS quoted_currency text;\n+\n+UPDATE public.single_session_bookings\n+SET booker_timezone = COALESCE(booker_timezone, 'America/Toronto')\n+WHERE booker_timezone IS NULL;\n+\n+ALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n+  ALTER COLUMN booker_timezone SET DEFAULT 'America/Toronto',\n+  ALTER COLUMN booker_timezone SET NOT NULL;\n+\n+ALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\n+  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS single_session_quoted_amount_nonnegative_chk,\n+  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_quoted_amount_nonnegative_chk\n+    CHECK (quoted_amount_cents IS NULL OR quoted_amount_cents >= 0);\n+\n+-- Keep the proven slot-claim transaction intact behind a private compatibility\n+-- function, then atomically attach the validated display timezone and immutable\n+-- checkout quote to the returned booking.\n+ALTER FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(\n+  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text\n+) RENAME TO claim_single_session_slot_without_confirmation_details;\n+\n+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot_without_confirmation_details(\n+  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text\n+) FROM public, anon, authenticated, service_role;\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(\n+  _start_at timestamptz,\n+  _name text,\n+  _email text,\n+  _notes text,\n+  _guests jsonb,\n+  _client_idempotency_key text,\n+  _booking_token_hash text,\n+  _ip_hash text,\n+  _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,\n+  _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session',\n+  _booker_timezone text DEFAULT 'America/Toronto'\n+) RETURNS public.single_session_bookings\n+LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\n+SET search_path = public\n+AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n+  _timezone text := NULLIF(trim(_booker_timezone), '');\n+  _amount integer;\n+  _currency text;\n+BEGIN\n+  IF _timezone IS NULL OR NOT EXISTS (\n+    SELECT 1 FROM pg_catalog.pg_timezone_names WHERE name = _timezone\n+  ) THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_timezone' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  _booking := public.claim_single_session_slot_without_confirmation_details(\n+    _start_at, _name, _email, _notes, _guests, _client_idempotency_key,\n+    _booking_token_hash, _ip_hash, _user_id, _event_slug\n+  );\n+\n+  _amount := public.booking_price_cents(_booking.event_slug, _booking.email);\n+  SELECT lower(currency) INTO _currency\n+  FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n+  IF _amount IS NULL OR _currency IS NULL THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_event_price' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b\n+  SET booker_timezone = COALESCE(b.booker_timezone, _timezone),\n+      quoted_amount_cents = COALESCE(b.quoted_amount_cents, _amount),\n+      quoted_currency = COALESCE(b.quoted_currency, _currency)\n+  WHERE b.id = _booking.id\n+  RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n+\n+  RETURN _booking;\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(\n+  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text, text\n+) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(\n+  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid, text, text\n+) TO service_role;\n+\n+-- One capability-protected status contract for paid checkout redirects and\n+-- free bookings, which have no Stripe checkout session.\n+DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.get_single_session_status(text, text);\n+\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_single_session_status(\n+  _booking_token_hash text,\n+  _stripe_checkout_session_id text DEFAULT NULL,\n+  _booking_id uuid DEFAULT NULL\n+) RETURNS jsonb\n+LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\n+SET search_path = public\n+AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n+  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n+  _calendar_url text;\n+  _calendar_ready boolean;\n+BEGIN\n+  IF NULLIF(_booking_token_hash, '') IS NULL\n+     OR (NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL AND _booking_id IS NULL) THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_booking_token' USING ERRCODE = 'P0403';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT * INTO _booking\n+  FROM public.single_session_bookings b\n+  WHERE b.booking_token_hash = _booking_token_hash\n+    AND (\n+      (_booking_id IS NOT NULL AND b.id = _booking_id)\n+      OR (NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NOT NULL\n+          AND b.stripe_checkout_session_id = _stripe_checkout_session_id)\n+    );\n+  IF NOT FOUND OR _booking.booking_token_expires_at <= now() THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_booking_token' USING ERRCODE = 'P0403';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n+  SELECT se.html_link INTO _calendar_url\n+  FROM public.schedule_events se\n+  WHERE se.google_event_id = _booking.gcal_event_id\n+    AND se.deleted_at IS NULL\n+  LIMIT 1;\n+\n+  _calendar_ready := _booking.status = 'booked'\n+    AND NULLIF(_booking.meet_url, '') IS NOT NULL\n+    AND NULLIF(_calendar_url, '') IS NOT NULL;\n+\n+  RETURN jsonb_strip_nulls(jsonb_build_object(\n+    'id', _booking.id,\n+    'booking_id', _booking.id,\n+    'status', _booking.status,\n+    'start_at', _booking.start_at,\n+    'end_at', _booking.end_at,\n+    'attendee_name', _booking.name,\n+    'event_label', COALESCE(_event.label, 'LSAT Session'),\n+    'duration_minutes', round(extract(epoch FROM (_booking.end_at - _booking.start_at)) / 60)::integer,\n+    'timezone', COALESCE(_booking.booker_timezone, 'America/Toronto'),\n+    'meet_url', CASE WHEN _booking.status = 'booked' THEN _booking.meet_url ELSE NULL END,\n+    'calendar_url', CASE WHEN _booking.status = 'booked' THEN _calendar_url ELSE NULL END,\n+    'calendar_ready', _calendar_ready,\n+    'calendar_pending', (_booking.status = 'booked' AND NOT _calendar_ready)\n+  ));\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_single_session_status(text, text, uuid)\n+  FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_single_session_status(text, text, uuid)\n+  TO service_role;\n+\n+-- Validate the paid event against the amount quoted when the hold was claimed.\n+-- Existing in-flight rows fall back to the current event/override resolver.\n+CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(\n+  _booking_id uuid,\n+  _provider_event_id text,\n+  _stripe_checkout_session_id text,\n+  _stripe_payment_intent_id text,\n+  _amount_cents integer,\n+  _currency text,\n+  _livemode boolean,\n+  _live_calendar_clear boolean,\n+  _cancel_token_hash text,\n+  _cancel_payload_ciphertext text\n+) RETURNS jsonb\n+LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER\n+SET search_path = public\n+AS $$\n+DECLARE\n+  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\n+  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\n+  _claimed_booking_id uuid;\n+  _can_book boolean := false;\n+  _new_version integer;\n+  _slot_start timestamptz;\n+  _expected_amount integer;\n+  _expected_currency text;\n+BEGIN\n+  IF NULLIF(_provider_event_id, '') IS NULL\n+     OR NULLIF(_stripe_checkout_session_id, '') IS NULL\n+     OR NULLIF(_stripe_payment_intent_id, '') IS NULL\n+     OR _amount_cents IS NULL OR _amount_cents < 0\n+     OR NULLIF(lower(COALESCE(_currency, '')), '') IS NULL\n+     OR _livemode IS NULL THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_payment_facts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT start_at INTO _slot_start FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id;\n+  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking_not_found' USING ERRCODE = 'P0404'; END IF;\n+  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_slot_start));\n+  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking_id FOR UPDATE;\n+  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _booking.event_slug;\n+  IF NOT FOUND OR _event.requires_payment IS DISTINCT FROM true THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  _expected_amount := COALESCE(\n+    _booking.quoted_amount_cents,\n+    public.booking_price_cents(_booking.event_slug, _booking.email)\n+  );\n+  _expected_currency := lower(COALESCE(_booking.quoted_currency, _event.currency));\n+  IF _amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _expected_amount\n+     OR lower(_currency) IS DISTINCT FROM _expected_currency THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_payment_facts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  SELECT single_session_booking_id INTO _claimed_booking_id\n+  FROM public.booking_commerce_events\n+  WHERE provider = 'stripe' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id\n+    AND event_type = 'single_session_paid';\n+  IF FOUND AND _claimed_booking_id IS DISTINCT FROM _booking_id THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'provider_event_already_claimed' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF _booking.stripe_checkout_session_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_checkout_session_id THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'checkout_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF _booking.status IN ('booked', 'cancelled', 'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed') THEN\n+    IF _booking.stripe_payment_intent_id IS DISTINCT FROM _stripe_payment_intent_id\n+       OR _booking.amount_cents IS DISTINCT FROM _amount_cents\n+       OR lower(_booking.currency) IS DISTINCT FROM lower(_currency)\n+       OR _booking.livemode IS DISTINCT FROM _livemode THEN\n+      RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_facts_mismatch' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n+    END IF;\n+    INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n+      provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n+      payload, single_session_booking_id\n+    ) VALUES (\n+      'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', _booking.event_slug,\n+      _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n+      jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n+      _booking_id\n+    ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n+    RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n+      'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', true);\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  IF _booking.status NOT IN ('checkout_open', 'expired', 'abandoned') THEN\n+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'payment_not_finalizable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0409';\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (\n+    provider, provider_event_id, event_type, offer_id, amount_cents, currency,\n+    payload, single_session_booking_id\n+  ) VALUES (\n+    'stripe', _provider_event_id, 'single_session_paid', _booking.event_slug,\n+    _amount_cents, lower(_currency),\n+    jsonb_build_object('livemode', _livemode, 'checkout_session_id', _stripe_checkout_session_id),\n+    _booking_id\n+  ) ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\n+\n+  _can_book := _live_calendar_clear\n+    AND (_booking.status = 'checkout_open'\n+      OR (_booking.status = 'expired' AND _booking.status_changed_at >= now() - interval '10 minutes'))\n+    AND public.booking_slot_is_clear(\n+      _booking.start_at, _booking.end_at,\n+      COALESCE(_event.buffer_before_minutes, 0), COALESCE(_event.buffer_after_minutes, 0),\n+      _booking.id, _booking.gcal_event_id, NULL\n+    );\n+\n+  IF _can_book THEN\n+    IF NULLIF(_cancel_token_hash, '') IS NULL OR NULLIF(_cancel_payload_ciphertext, '') IS NULL THEN\n+      RAISE EXCEPTION 'cancel_capability_required' USING ERRCODE = 'P0400';\n+    END IF;\n+    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n+      status = 'booked', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n+      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n+      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n+      cancel_token_hash = _cancel_token_hash, cancel_token_expires_at = b.start_at,\n+      terminal_reason = NULL\n+    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n+    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n+\n+    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_promote', 'system', 'pending',\n+      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n+    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_booking_confirmation', 'booker', 'blocked',\n+      'booked', _new_version, _cancel_payload_ciphertext);\n+    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'email_admin_notification', 'admin', 'blocked',\n+      'booked', _new_version, NULL);\n+  ELSE\n+    UPDATE public.single_session_bookings b SET\n+      status = 'refund_pending', lifecycle_version = b.lifecycle_version + 1,\n+      stripe_payment_intent_id = _stripe_payment_intent_id,\n+      amount_cents = _amount_cents, currency = lower(_currency), livemode = _livemode,\n+      terminal_reason = 'paid_slot_unavailable'\n+    WHERE b.id = _booking_id RETURNING * INTO _booking;\n+    _new_version := _booking.lifecycle_version;\n+    UPDATE public.single_session_outbox SET state = 'superseded', updated_at = now()\n+      WHERE booking_id = _booking.id AND state IN ('blocked', 'pending', 'failed')\n+        AND action IN ('gcal_promote', 'email_booking_confirmation', 'email_admin_notification');\n+    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'gcal_delete', 'system', 'pending',\n+      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n+    PERFORM public.enqueue_single_session_outbox(_booking.id, 'stripe_refund', 'system', 'pending',\n+      'refund_pending', _new_version, NULL);\n+  END IF;\n+\n+  RETURN jsonb_build_object('booking_id', _booking.id, 'status', _booking.status,\n+    'lifecycle_version', _booking.lifecycle_version, 'was_existing', false);\n+END;\n+$$;\n+\n+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(\n+  uuid, text, text, text, integer, text, boolean, boolean, text, text\n+) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\n+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.finalize_single_session_payment(\n+  uuid, text, text, text, integer, text, boolean, boolean, text, text\n+) TO service_role;\n+\n*** End Patch";

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\r\n    _provider_status: options.providerStatus ?? null,\r\n    _result: options.result ?? {},\r\n    _meet_url: options.meetUrl ?? null,\r\n  });\r\n  if (error) throw error;\r\n  return data;\r\n}\r\n\r\nasync function fail(supabase: any, job: any, error: unknown, providerStatus?: string, confirmedFailure = false) {\r\n  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n  const { error: failureError } = await supabase.rpc("fail_single_session_outbox", {\r\n    _job_id: job.id,\r\n    _lease_token: job.lease_token,\r\n    _error: message,\r\n    _provider_status: providerStatus ?? null,\r\n    _confirmed_failure: confirmedFailure,\r\n  });\r\n  if (failureError && failureError.code !== "P0409") throw failureError;\r\n  return message;\r\n}\r\n\r\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\r\n  if (req.method !== "POST") return json({ error: "method_not_allowed" }, 405);\r\n  try {\r\n    const declared = Number(req.headers.get("content-length") || "0");\r\n    if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return json({ error: "payload_too_large" }, 413);\r\n    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n    // Two accepted callers: a worker holding BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET, and the\r\n    // scheduled job, which presents a single-use ticket only a service-role\r\n    // reader could have minted. No shared secret lives in SQL or cron metadata.\r\n    if (!authorize(req) && !(await authorizeTicket(supabase, req))) {\r\n      return json({ error: "unauthorized" }, 401);\r\n    }\r\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "start");\r\n    const workerId = `${WORKER_NAME}:${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\r\n    const results: { id: string; action: string; outcome: string }[] = [];\r\n    // A calendar promotion unblocks its email jobs. Claim twice, and only\r\n    // twice, so those emails can send in this invocation without turning one\r\n    // request into an unbounded queue drain.\r\n    for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {\r\n      const { data: jobs, error: claimError } = await supabase.rpc("claim_single_session_outbox", {\r\n        _worker_id: workerId,\r\n        _actions: null,\r\n        _limit: 20,\r\n        _lease_seconds: 180,\r\n      });\r\n      if (claimError) throw claimError;\r\n      if (!jobs?.length) break;\r\n      for (const job of jobs) {\r\n      const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from("single_session_bookings")\r\n        .select("*").eq("id", job.booking_id).maybeSingle();\r\n      if (bookingError || !booking) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, bookingError ?? new Error("booking_not_found"));\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n      if (booking.status !== job.precondition_status || booking.lifecycle_version !== job.precondition_lifecycle_version) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, new Error("lifecycle_precondition_changed"));\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "superseded" });\r\n        continue;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      try {\r\n        if (job.action === "gcal_promote") {\r\n          if (!booking.gcal_event_id) throw new Error("tentative_event_missing");\r\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n          const targetCalendar = state?.calendar_id || "primary";\r\n          const attendees = [booking.email, ...((booking.guests ?? []) as string[])].filter(Boolean).map((email) => ({ email }));\r\n          let event = await patchEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id, {\r\n            summary: `${String(booking.name || "Student").trim().split(/\\s+/)[0]} session`,\r\n            description: booking.notes ? `Booked via LSAT U.\\n\\nNotes from Booker:\\n${booking.notes}` : "Booked via LSAT U.",\r\n            attendees,\r\n            visibility: "default",\r\n            conferenceData: { createRequest: { requestId: booking.id, conferenceSolutionKey: { type: "hangoutsMeet" } } },\r\n            extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_single_session_booking_id: booking.id, lsatu_hold: "false" } },\r\n          }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n          let meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\r\n          if (!meetUrl) {\r\n            event = await getEvent(targetCalendar, booking.gcal_event_id);\r\n            meetUrl = meetUrlFromEvent(event);\r\n          }\r\n          if (!meetUrl) throw new Error("meet_url_pending");\r\n          if (!event.htmlLink) throw new Error("calendar_url_pending");\r\n          const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").upsert({\r\n            google_event_id: event.id,\r\n            calendar_id: targetCalendar,\r\n            title: event.summary,\r\n            description: event.description,\r\n            start_at: booking.start_at,\r\n            end_at: booking.end_at,\r\n            all_day: false,\r\n            status: event.status ?? "confirmed",\r\n            html_link: event.htmlLink ?? null,\r\n            meet_url: meetUrl,\r\n            source: "single_session",\r\n            etag: event.etag ?? null,\r\n            last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n            deleted_at: null,\r\n          }, { onConflict: "google_event_id" });\r\n          if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n          const completion = await complete(supabase, job, {\r\n            meetUrl,\r\n            result: { google_event_id: event.id, calendar_url: event.htmlLink },\r\n          });\r\n          if (completion?.compensate) {\r\n            await deleteEvent(targetCalendar, event.id, { sendUpdates: "all" });\r\n            await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({ status: "cancelled", deleted_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("google_event_id", event.id);\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (job.action === "gcal_delete") {\r\n          const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from("gcal_sync_state").select("calendar_id").eq("id", 1).maybeSingle();\r\n          if (stateError) throw stateError;\r\n          const eventId = booking.gcal_event_id;\r\n          if (eventId) {\r\n            await deleteEvent(state?.calendar_id || "primary", eventId, { sendUpdates: booking.status === "cancelled" ? "all" : "none" });\r\n            const { error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from("schedule_events").update({\r\n              status: "cancelled",\r\n              deleted_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n              last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\r\n            }).eq("google_event_id", eventId);\r\n            if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\r\n          }\r\n          await complete(supabase, job, { result: { google_event_id: eventId ?? null } });\r\n        } else if (job.action === "stripe_refund") {\r\n          if (!booking.stripe_payment_intent_id) throw new Error("payment_intent_missing");\r\n          if (!Number.isInteger(Number(booking.amount_cents)) || Number(booking.amount_cents) <= 0) {\r\n            throw new Error("refund_amount_missing");\r\n          }\r\n          const stripeSecret = requiredEnv("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY");\r\n          const refund = booking.stripe_refund_id\r\n            ? await retrieveRefund(stripeSecret, booking.stripe_refund_id)\r\n            : await createRefund(stripeSecret, {\r\n              payment_intent: booking.stripe_payment_intent_id,\r\n              amount: Number(booking.amount_cents),\r\n              metadata: { booking_id: booking.id, product: "single_session" },\r\n            }, job.idempotency_key);\r\n          if (refund.status === "succeeded") {\r\n            await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: refund.status, result: { refund_id: refund.id } });\r\n          } else if (["failed", "canceled"].includes(String(refund.status))) {\r\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_confirmed_failed"), String(refund.status), true);\r\n          } else {\r\n            await fail(supabase, job, new Error("stripe_refund_pending"), String(refund.status), false);\r\n          }\r\n        } else if (job.action.startsWith("email_")) {\r\n          const payload = job.payload_ciphertext\r\n            ? await decryptEnvelope(job.payload_ciphertext, requiredEnv("SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY"))\r\n            : {};\r\n          const [{ data: eventType, error: eventTypeError }, { data: calendarEvent, error: calendarEventError }] = await Promise.all([\r\n            supabase.from("booking_event_types").select("label").eq("slug", booking.event_slug).maybeSingle(),\r\n            booking.gcal_event_id\r\n              ? supabase.from("schedule_events").select("html_link").eq("google_event_id", booking.gcal_event_id).maybeSingle()\r\n              : Promise.resolve({ data: null, error: null }),\r\n          ]);\r\n          if (eventTypeError) throw eventTypeError;\r\n          if (calendarEventError) throw calendarEventError;\r\n          await sendEmail(job, {\r\n            ...booking,\r\n            event_label: eventType?.label || "LSAT Session",\r\n            calendar_url: calendarEvent?.html_link || null,\r\n          }, payload);\r\n          await complete(supabase, job, { providerStatus: "accepted" });\r\n        } else {\r\n          throw new Error("unknown_outbox_action");\r\n        }\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "processed" });\r\n      } catch (error) {\r\n        await fail(supabase, job, error);\r\n        console.error("single-session outbox job failed", { bookingId: booking.id, jobId: job.id, action: job.action, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\r\n        results.push({ id: job.id, action: job.action, outcome: "retry" });\r\n      }\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n    await heartbeat(supabase, "success", undefined, { processed: results.length });\r\n    return json({ processed: results.length, results });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\r\n    try {\r\n      const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv("SUPABASE_URL"), requiredEnv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"));\r\n      await heartbeat(supabase, "error", message);\r\n    } catch (_) { /* fail-closed response is the durable alert when heartbeat is unavailable */ }\r\n    console.error("single-session-outbox error", { error: message });\r\n    return json({ error: "worker_failed" }, 500);\r\n  }\r\n});\r\n'}]

## assistant

Implemented the booking backend changes.

Changed:

- `supabase/functions/single-session/index.ts`
- `supabase/functions/single-session-outbox/index.ts`
- `supabase/migrations/20260915090000_booking_confirmation_details.sql`
- `src/lib/bookingConfirmationBackendContract.test.ts`

Key results:

- Persists validated booker timezone and immutable quoted price.
- Supports protected status lookup by Stripe session or booking ID.
- Returns attendee, event label, duration, timezone, calendar URL, and readiness.
- Requires both Google Meet and Calendar URLs before promotion completes.
- Immediately nudges free bookings and performs a bounded second outbox claim for newly unblocked emails.
- Sends dynamic booking details into the email template.
- Refunds the actual paid amount.
- Replaces the hardcoded $45 SQL validation with booking-specific pricing.

Verification:

- 11 targeted tests passed.
- Both Edge Functions passed `deno check`.
- Full TypeScript checking is currently blocked by concurrent email-template files using Deno `npm:` imports, unrelated to these backend changes.

Remaining integration: `booking-commerce-webhook/index.ts` still validates against the event’s base price before reaching SQL. Another owner must update that file for email-specific price overrides. A $1 one-off event whose event price is $1 already works with these changes.

## assistant

I’ll clean only the two Edge Functions back to their original formatting, then reapply the backend behavior as small patches. The migration, tests, frontend, email, and other concurrent files will remain untouched.

supabase/migrations/20260915170000_booking_confirmation_details.sql\r\nimport { readFileSync } from "node:fs";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";\n\nconst read = (path: string) => readFileSync(path, "utf8");\n\ndescribe("booking confirmation backend contracts", () => {\n  const migrationPath = "supabase/migrations/20260915170000_booking_confirmation_details.sql";\n\n  it("protects enriched status with the booking capability and supports free bookings", () => {\n    const sql = read(migrationPath);\n    expect(sql).toContain("b.booking_token_hash = _booking_token_hash");\n    expect(sql).toContain("_booking_id IS NOT NULL AND b.id = _booking_id");\n    expect(sql).toContain("\'attendee_name\'");\n    expect(sql).toContain("\'calendar_url\'");\n    expect(sql).toContain("\'calendar_ready\'");\n    expect(sql).toContain("\'calendar_pending\'");\n  });\n\n  it("persists the timezone and immutable event-specific quote at claim time", () => {\n    const sql = read(migrationPath);\n    const endpoint = read("supabase/functions/single-session/index.ts");\n    expect(sql).toContain("booker_timezone");\n    expect(sql).toContain("quoted_amount_cents");\n    expect(sql).toContain("public.booking_price_cents(_booking.event_slug, _booking.email)");\n    expect(endpoint).toContain("_booker_timezone: String(body.timezone");\n    expect(endpoint).toContain("amountCents");\n  });\n\n  it("requires both Google destinations and immediately drains unblocked email work", () => {\n    const worker = read("supabase/functions/single-session-outbox/index.ts");\n    expect(worker).toContain(\'if (!meetUrl || !event.htmlLink)\');\n    expect(worker).toContain(\'throw new Error("calendar_url_pending")\');\n    expect(worker).toContain("for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++)");\n    expect(worker).toContain("amount: Number(booking.amount_cents)");\n  });\n});\n\r\n\r\n'}]

## assistant

Fixed both reviewer issues:

- First claims now persist the validated timezone, while idempotent replays preserve the original timezone and quoted price.
- Stripe webhook validation now uses immutable quoted amount/currency.
- Legacy bookings use the override-aware price resolver before falling back to the event price.

Verification:

- 12 focused tests passed.
- Single-session and outbox Deno checks passed.
- Diff checks passed.
- The webhook still has unrelated pre-existing Stripe type errors outside the changed block.
